--c15t-primary: hsl(228, 100%, 60%);

--c15t-surface-hover: hsl(0, 0%, 98%);

--c15t-switch-track: hsl(0, 0%, 85%);
--c15t-switch-track-active: hsl(228, 100%, 60%);

--c15t-easing-spring: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
}

:root.dark, .dark .c15t-theme-root, :root.c15t-dark, .c15t-dark .c15t-theme-root {

/*
 * Utility class to disable transitions during theme switching.
 * Apply this class to the root element before switching themes,
 * then remove it after a short delay to prevent flash of
 * animated content during the color scheme change.
 *
 * Example usage:
 *   document.documentElement.classList.add('c15t-no-transitions');
 *   // Switch theme...
 *   requestAnimationFrame(() => {
 *     document.documentElement.classList.remove('c15t-no-transitions');
 *   });
 */
.c15t-no-transitions,
.c15t-no-transitions *,
.c15t-no-transitions *::before,
.c15t-no-transitions *::after {
	transition: none !important;
	animation: none !important;

max-w-xl flex-col items-center gap-1 mb-12"><h2 class="font-plex-serif text-balance scroll-mt-24 h2 high-contrast-text mb-2 text-center">Predicting intent, preserving focus</h2><p class="tracking-tight text-center text-balance">Edit predictions that fit into your existing workflow. Other tools complete code. Zed predicts what you&#x27;re trying to do next.</p></hgroup><div class="group/cards grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-12"><div class="group/media relative flex min-h-fit w-full flex-col gap-4 transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out will-change-opacity pointer-fine:group-has-[.card-item:hover]/cards:opacity-35 hover:!opacity-100 card-item"><div class="group group/info-bar group/media transform-translate relative overflow-clip duration-70 default-border-color rounded-xs border"><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="3280" height="2160" decoding="async" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 1024px) calc(100vw - Once you accept a prediction, you can perform multiple follow-up edits by pressing tab repeatedly, saving you time and keystrokes.</p></div></div><div class="group/media relative flex min-h-fit w-full flex-col gap-4 transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out will-change-opacity pointer-fine:group-has-[.card-item:hover]/cards:opacity-35 hover:!opacity-100 card-item"><div class="group group/info-bar group/media transform-translate relative overflow-clip duration-70 default-border-color rounded-xs border"><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="3280" height="2160" decoding="async" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 1024px) calc(100vw - As soon as you press the modifier, Zed previews the edit and hides the menu to enable an unobstructed review.</p></div></div><div class="group/media relative flex min-h-fit w-full flex-col gap-4 transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out will-change-opacity pointer-fine:group-has-[.card-item:hover]/cards:opacity-35 hover:!opacity-100 card-item"><div class="group group/info-bar group/media transform-translate relative overflow-clip duration-70 default-border-color rounded-xs border"><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="3280" height="2160" decoding="async" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 1024px) calc(100vw -
